*So Hasegawa,Shailaja Keyur Sampat,Lei Liu,Wei-Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DataGovBench 是一个基于政府开放数据的基准，用于评估 LLMs 在实际数据分析场景中的表现，包含表格问答和表格洞察两个任务，实验显示当前最先进 LLMs 与真实需求仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦于小表格的事实检索，忽视了大型多表数据集、外部知识整合以及探索性洞察发现等真实世界数据分析的核心挑战。

Method: 构建 DataGovBench 基准，设计 Table QA（需解决复杂可分解问题并生成文本答案或可视化）和 Table Insight（需通过探索性数据分析生成专家级发现）两个任务，在是否引入智能体框架的条件下对多个最先进 LLMs 进行全面实验。

Result: 实验结果表明，最先进 LLMs 在两个任务上均表现出显著的性能差距，无论是否使用智能体框架，均难以满足真实世界数据分析的需求。

Conclusion: DataGovBench 为推动既能回答分析查询又能从数据中自主发现洞察的 LLMs 研究提供了具有挑战性的评测平台。

Abstract: Current benchmarks for evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) in data analysis often fail to reflect real-world settings. They typically focus on fact retrieval from small tables and overlook the challenges of large multi-tabular datasets, external knowledge integration, and exploratory insight discovery. We introduce DataGovBench, a benchmark derived from governmental open data designed to evaluate LLMs in practical scenarios. The benchmark includes two tasks: Table QA that requires solving complex decomposable questions and producing textual answers or visualizations, and Table Insight that evaluates the ability of models to generate expert-level findings through exploratory data analysis. Comprehensive experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs, both with and without agentic frameworks, reveal significant performance gaps across both tasks. These results suggest that current LLM-based systems remain far from satisfying the demands of real-world data analytics. DataGovBench provides a challenging benchmark for advancing research on LLMs capable of both answering analytical queries and discovering insights from data. Code and sample data are available at https://github.com/SoHasegawa/datagovbench.

</details>


### [36] [Pitwall: Faithful Natural-Language Race-Strategy Briefings from a Calibrated Real-Time Monte Carlo Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06495)
*Juan S. Santillana*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Pitwall 是一个生产级系统，能够实时生成英语、西班牙语和葡萄牙语的 F1 赛事策略简报，将忠实性视为架构属性而非事后追求：每句话拆解为类型化事实声明并逐条针对概率赛况状态验证，训练数据同样由验证器把关，仅保留 81.9% 全程可溯源的目标文本。底层基于 2000 路向量化蒙特卡洛赛况延拓引擎（126 场历史赛事标定，2025-2026 赛季 held-out 验证 winner-in-top-3 90.3%、Brier 0.0745），实现了从实时计时到三语言经验证简报的端到端落地，并在 2026 奥地利、英国两站实测中提前 10 圈锁定冠军概率。核心发现：校准最优≠决策最优、丰富目标微调会在稀疏上下文导致幻觉（源于基座模型指令遵循而非规模），需分门别类门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现场体育解说属于“带截止期的 grounded generation”：实体真实且具名、场景每几秒变化、生成时无参考文本。现有方法多将忠实性作为事后指标或 aspiration，难以在实时、多语言、高动态的 F1 策略简报场景提供可验证的事实保障。

Method: 1. 构建向量化蒙特卡洛引擎（N=2000 per-lap 赛况延拓），用 126 场 2018-2024 赛事标定，在 2025-2026 举出赛季验证（winner-in-top-3 90.3%，Brier 0.0745）。2. 将每句生成句子分解为六类类型化事实声明（位置、间隙、轮胎、节奏、超车、赛控），逐条针对概率赛况状态验证。3. 同一验证器把关微调数据：3045 条模型生成目标中仅保留 81.9% 全声明可溯源样本，其余回退至可证明忠实的模板，确保生成器从未见过无依据目标。4. 部署稀疏上下文审计机制，剔除基座模型指令遵循导致的幻觉。

Result: 端到端系统在 2026 奥地利站、英国站连续两场实测通过；银石站实测中，落盘的时间戳概率轨迹在比赛结束前 10 圈即锁定最终冠军。模拟实验显示校准最优不等于决策最优；生成侧实验证实：更丰富的微调目标虽提升生动度，但在状态稀疏时会崩塌为幻觉，溯源至基座模型指令遵循而非模型规模，稀疏上下文审计可从生产模型中移除该失效模式。

Conclusion: Pitwall 证明了将忠实性作为架构不变量（而非事后指标）并配合可验证的概率赛况底座，可实现实时、多语言、高动态体育赛事的可信自然语言生成。核心经验：不同美德（校准、决策、生动、忠实）存在权衡，必须分别显式门控；稀疏上下文审计是抑制基座模型指令遵循导致幻觉的关键手段。

Abstract: Live sports commentary is grounded generation under a deadline: statements concern real, named athletes, the grounding state changes every few seconds, and no reference text exists at generation time. We present Pitwall, a production system that generates natural-language Formula 1 strategy briefings in English, Spanish, and Portuguese, treating faithfulness as an architectural property rather than an aspiration: every published sentence is decomposed into typed factual claims (positions, gaps, tyres, pace, overtakes, race control) and each claim is verified against the probabilistic race state that prompted it. The same verifier gates the fine-tuning data: of 3,045 model-written targets, only the 81.9% whose every claim is state-supported are retained, the rest falling back to a provably faithful template, so the generator never sees an ungrounded target. Verification is meaningful because of the grounding substrate: a vectorized Monte Carlo engine (N=2,000 per-lap race continuations) calibrated on 126 races (2018-2024) and validated on fully held-out 2025-2026 seasons (winner-in-top-3 90.3% over 155 backtests; held-out Brier 0.0745). A recurring finding spans both halves of the system: virtues trade off and must be gated separately. In simulation, calibration-optimal is not decision-optimal; in generation, fine-tuning on richer targets buys vividness that collapses into hallucination when the grounding state is sparse -- a failure a four-base replication traces to base-model instruction adherence, not scale, and that sparse-context auditing removes from the production model. End-to-end operation -- live timing to verified trilingual briefings -- was confirmed at two consecutive live Grands Prix (Austria and Britain, 2026); at Silverstone a timestamped probability trace, committed to disk before the outcome was known, locked onto the eventual winner ten laps before the flag.

</details>


### [37] [RSF-GLLM: Bridging the Semantic Gap in Multi-Hop Knowledge Graph QA via Recurrent Soft-Flow and Decoupled LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06527)
*Sambaran Bandyopadhyay,Ananth Muppidi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出RSF-GLLM框架，通过可微图推理与答案生成解耦，利用GRU引导的软流传播和稀疏正则化实现多跳知识图谱问答，在WebQSP和CWQ上达到了有竞争力的性能且推理效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的检索-阅读流程破坏了可微分性，导致检索器无法学习跨越语义鸿沟（中间节点与查询缺乏词汇重叠）的能力，因此需要一种可微分的图推理方法来解决多跳问答中的语义鸿沟问题。

Method: 提出RSF-GLLM框架，包含Recurrent Soft-Flow (RSF)模块：使用GRU引导的查询更新器传播连续相关性分数，动态门控机制利用结构线索遍历语义不相似的桥接节点；引入流稀疏正则化理论保证从软概率收敛到离散推理路径；提取并文本化路径微调LLM，确保生成基于事实拓扑。

Result: 在WebQSP和CWQ数据集上实验表明，RSF-GLLM性能有竞争力，且推理效率优于基于LLM的计算密集型方法。

Conclusion: RSF-GLLM通过可微分图推理与LLM生成解耦，有效解决了多跳知识图谱问答中的语义鸿沟问题，同时保持了高推理效率，为复杂知识图谱推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multi-hop Question Answering over Knowledge Graphs faces a critical challenge: traditional retrieve-then-read pipelines break differentiability, preventing the retriever from learning to bridge the semantic gap where intermediate nodes lack lexical overlap with the query. To address this, we propose RSF-GLLM, a framework decoupling differentiable graph reasoning from answer generation. Our Recurrent Soft-Flow (RSF) module employs a GRU-guided query updater to propagate continuous relevance scores, utilizing a dynamic gating mechanism to traverse semantically dissimilar bridge nodes via structural cues. We introduce flow sparsity regularization to theoretically guarantee convergence from soft probabilities to discrete reasoning paths. These paths are extracted and textualized to fine-tune a Large Language Model (LLM), ensuring generation is grounded in factual topology. Experiments on WebQSP and CWQ demonstrate that RSF-GLLM achieves competitive performance with superior inference efficiency compared to LLM based computationally expensive approaches.

</details>


### [38] [Life Style Levels: Neighborhood Delineation using Geospatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06529)
*Srivatsa Kulkarni,Debarag Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于建筑形态的网格化城市划分框架，利用开源卫星影像对印度59个城市进行高分辨率网格划分，通过可解释的形态指标和规则评分体系识别城市富裕程度差异，并经街景验证及孟买非正规聚落聚类分析确认有效性，最后结合消费贷款违约数据探索性映射，实现低成本、可扩展的细粒度城市富裕度映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展中国家快速城市化地区缺乏细尺度社会经济数据，难以刻画城市内部富裕与剥夺的空间差异，亟需一种可扩展、低成本且可解释的方法来获取细粒度城市富裕度信息。

Method: 利用开源卫星影像提取建筑足迹，将59个印度城市划分为高分辨率网格，计算可解释的建筑形态指标（如密度、大小、形状等），构建透明的基于规则的评分框架划分富裕等级；通过Google Street View街景验证分类结果；在孟买采用基于密度的建筑足迹聚类识别非正规聚落；最后将消费贷款违约率映射到富裕等级进行探索性分析。

Result: 网格分类在街景观测中显示出显著的富裕度差异，符合预期的生活方式指标；孟买建筑足迹聚类与已知非正规聚落空间重叠度高；消费贷款违约率在不同富裕等级间呈现明显分层，验证了框架的实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架完全依赖公开地理空间数据，提供了一种可扩展、可解释、低成本的细粒度城市富裕度映射方法，可为印度及类似快速城市化地区的政策制定和资源分配提供数据支持。

Abstract: Fine-scale socioeconomic information is often unavailable across rapidly ur-banizing regions of the developing world, like India, limiting the ability to delineate intra-urban variations in affluence and deprivation. This study pro-poses a scalable, grid-based urban delineation framework using building morphology derived from open-source satellite imagery. Urban areas across 59 Indian cities and towns are partitioned into high-resolution spatial grids and characterized using interpretable morphological indicators, which are combined into a transparent, rule-based scoring framework to delineate areas with contrasting levels of urban affluence. The resulting classifications are validated through ground-level Google Street View observations, revealing a sharp contrast between the grid classes which are consistent with the ex-pected effects of the lifestyle affluence indicators. We further investigate density-based clustering of building footprints in Mumbai to identify dense urban settlements, demonstrating that the resulting clusters exhibit substan-tial spatial overlap with known informal settlements across the city. Finally, we conduct an exploratory analysis mapping consumer loan delinquency across the derived affluence classes. By relying entirely on publicly available geospatial data, the proposed framework provides a scalable, interpretable, and cost-effective approach for granular urban affluence mapping across In-dian cities.

</details>


### [39] [Hierarchical Acoustic-Semantic Modeling: Modality Separation and Semantic Coherence for Full-Duplex SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06540)
*Zhenyu Liu,Yunxin Li,Xuanyu Zhang,Qixun Teng,Shenyuan Jiang,Haolan Chen,Minjun Zhao,Fanbo Meng,Yu Xu,Yancheng He,Baotian Hu,Haizhou Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Lychee-FD，一种原生端到端全双工语音语言模型框架，通过层级参数分离策略解决声学与语义建模的梯度冲突，显著提升语音智能和全双工交互流畅度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工 SLM 受模态干扰限制，导致知识退化和语义完整性受损，根本原因是声学与语义建模在共享深层参数空间时产生的梯度冲突。

Method: 提出层级参数分离策略，在深层解耦冲突模态，并通过专用语义对齐通道保持跨模态一致性，构建原生端到端全双工框架 Lychee-FD。

Result: 在多个全双工基准上实验，Spoken QA 提升 7.4%，FullDuplexBench 1.5 提升 28.5%，推理效率不降低。

Conclusion: 首次揭示全双工 SLM 模态干扰的根本原因，并设计优雅的层级模型与实用方案，实现无缝、高性能、原生智能的全双工 SLM。

Abstract: Developing seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex Spoken Language Models (SLMs) remains a critical challenge and long-standing goal for the speech and NLP community. Despite notable progress, recent endeavors are fundamentally constrained by severe modality interference, which causes substantial knowledge degradation and compromises semantic integrity -- ultimately making full-duplex SLMs feel unnatural and unintelligent. In this paper, through an exhaustive fine-grained analysis of model optimization dynamics, we uncover the root cause of such performance degradation, revealing that modality interference arises from inherent gradient conflicts between acoustic and semantic modeling when the two modalities are forced to share a deep parameter space. Guided by this key insight, we introduce Lychee-FD, a native end-to-end full-duplex framework designed to mitigate modality interference. Importantly, we propose a hierarchical parameter separation strategy that decouples conflicting modalities in deep layers while preserving cross-modality coherence via a dedicated semantic alignment channel. Extensive experiments on multiple full-duplex benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly advances the state of the art, yielding substantial improvements in both speech intelligence (+7.4% on Spoken QA) and full-duplex interaction fluidity (+28.5% on FullDuplexBench 1.5) without compromising inference efficiency. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to achieve two key advances: 1) uncovering and elucidating the root cause of modality interference in full-duplex SLMs, and 2) designing an elegant hierarchical model together with a practical solution for seamless, high-performance, native intelligent full-duplex SLMs.

</details>


### [40] [On the feasibility of dependency parsing of non-human sequences without a gold standard. Is evaluation possible in other species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06542)
*Ramon Ferrer-i-Cancho,Catherine Hobaiter,Thore Bergman,Morgan Gustison*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文利用网络科学证明非人类灵长类动物发声/手势序列的快速长度衰减分布使得无监督依存句法分析无需金标准即可评估，而人类语言缺乏此特性，因此无金标准评估在非人类灵长类可行，在人类语言中困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无监督依存句法分析在无金标准物种（如非人类灵长类）中评估准确性的难题，挑战“无金标准即无法评估准确性”的传统观点。

Method: 应用网络科学的最新进展，分析序列长度分布的衰减特性（快速衰减 vs 慢速衰减），理论推导解析器正确边比例与序列长度分布的关系。

Result: 非人类灵长类动物的发声/手势序列长度分布快速衰减，导致解析器检索到的正确边比例必须很高；人类语言序列缺乏该特性，无金标准评估在非人类灵长类可行，在人类中困难。

Conclusion: 无监督依存句法分析在非人类灵长类动物序列中无需金标准即可评估准确性，而人类语言因序列长度分布特性不同导致该评估困难，揭示了物种间句法结构可评估性的根本差异。

Abstract: Dependency parsing consists of finding a tree representation for a sequence. Unsupervised dependency parsing aims to develop parsing methods without a gold standard during model training. In human languages, an unsupervised parser can be evaluated because some gold standard is usually available or can be created. For other species, a gold standard is unknown. Thus one may conclude that it is impossible to determine the accuracy of an unsupervised parser and, consequently, dependency parsing is unfeasible in other species. However, here we apply recent advances in network science to demonstrate that the proportion of correct edges retrieved by a parser must be high for the sequences of vocalizations or gestures that non-human primates produce due to the fast decay of the sequence length distribution. In contrast, human language sequences lack that property. Therefore, evaluation without a gold standard is feasible in non-human primates but a hard problem in humans.

</details>


### [41] [Diffusion Language Model Parallel Decoding via Product-of-Experts Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08048)
*Juntong Shi,Brian L. Trippe,Jure Leskovec,Stefano Ermon,Minkai Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PoE-Bridge 是一种新颖的解码框架，通过引入基于乘积专家的中间分布，结合扩散语言模型的并行解码优势和自回归模型的生成质量，实现了 5 倍加速且恢复 95% 以上的 AR 模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLM）虽具备并行解码的速度优势，但因缺乏 token 级依赖导致生成质量不如自回归（AR）模型。现有重要性采样方法虽试图弥合两者分布差距，但因分布鸿沟过大导致采样开销巨大，亟需更高效的桥接方案。

Method: 提出 PoE-Bridge 框架：构建 DLM 提议分布与 AR 目标分布的乘积专家（PoE）中间分布；先用 DLM 并行生成多个候选续写，再经拒绝采样将候选向 PoE 对齐，最后用重要性采样将 PoE 对齐的候选修正至 AR 目标；辅以混合温度采样增强多样性、弹性拒绝窗口减少无效验证。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成等高难度任务上，PoE-Bridge 相较标准 DLM 解码实现 5 倍加速，同时恢复至少 95% 的 AR 模型性能，大幅缩小质量鸿沟。

Conclusion: PoE-Bridge 通过中间分布桥接与多阶段采样策略，高效结合了 DLM 的并行速度与 AR 的生成质量，为扩散语言模型的实际部署提供了可行的高性能解码范式。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer substantial speed advantages through parallel decoding, but the lack of token dependencies limits generation quality compared to autoregressive (AR) models. Recent progress attempts to bridge the gap via importance sampling, with DLM being the proposal and AR being the target. However, due to the huge gap between their distributions, the sampling requires a large number of particles and is thus expensive to compute. In this paper, we introduce PoE-Bridge, a novel decoding framework that drastically improves generation speed and accuracy by introducing an intermediate distribution to bridge the gap. The distribution is constructed as a Product-of-Experts (PoE) of the DLM proposal and the AR target. With the intermediate distribution, we first use the DLM to draft multiple continuations in parallel, then apply rejection sampling to verify the drafted tokens and move the resulting candidates toward the PoE. We then use importance sampling to further correct the PoE-aligned candidates toward the AR target. We further propose several improved techniques, including mixed-temperature sampling for enhanced diversity and elastic rejection windows for reducing wasted verification. Empirically, PoE-Bridge achieves significantly improved accuracy with $5\times$ speedup over the standard DLM decoding approach, and recovers at least 95% of the target AR model's performance, efficiently advancing most of the quality gap on challenging mathematical reasoning and coding tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/juntongshi48/poe-bridge.

</details>


### [42] [Improving LLM-Generated Process Model Quality Through Reinforcement Learning: The Role of Reward Function Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06175)
*Alexander Rombach,Chantale Lauer,Nijat Mehdiyev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于强化学习的BPMN流程模型生成中的奖励函数设计，对比了Llama 3.1 8B和Qwen 2.5 14B两个模型家族在48种配置下的表现，发现：RL显著提升语用和句法质量并保持语义保真度，等权重奖励优于针对性加权，且奖励设计与模型架构存在非平凡交互作用，奖励组合是决定优化结果的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可从自然语言生成BPMN流程模型，但监督微调（SFT）将输出质量限制在训练数据模式内。强化学习（RL）可利用外部质量度量突破这一上限，然而当质量是多维度时，奖励函数应如何设计尚未被探索。

Method: 采用Group Sequence Policy Optimization（GSPO）训练Llama 3.1 8B和Qwen 2.5 14B两个模型家族，共48种配置。奖励源自包含38个指标的自动化评估框架，覆盖句法、语用和语义三个质量维度。系统实验对比不同奖励加权策略、无效惩罚、SFT初始化等设计选择。

Result: 1. RL显著提升语用和句法质量，同时保持语义保真度，输出变异性降低六倍以上。2. 等权重奖励持续优于针对性加权：强调特定维度反而无法改善该维度，甚至导致模型崩溃为低质量模式。3. 设计选择与模型架构非平凡交互：无效惩罚对一个模型至关重要，对另一个无关紧要；SFT初始化对一个架构不可或缺，对另一个却适得其反。

Conclusion: 奖励组合是决定优化结果的主要因素，其影响程度堪比是否应用RL本身。这些发现可推广到任何通过多个自动化维度评估质量的结构化生成任务。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate BPMN process models from natural-language descriptions, yet supervised fine-tuning (SFT) limits their output quality to the patterns present in the training data. Reinforcement learning (RL) can optimize beyond this ceiling using external quality measures, but how the reward function should be designed when quality is multi-dimensional remains unexplored. We present a systematic investigation of reward function design for RL-based process model generation, training two LLM families (Llama~3.1 8B, Qwen~2.5 14B) under 48 configurations using Group Sequence Policy Optimization with rewards derived from an automated evaluation framework comprising 38 metrics across syntactic, pragmatic, and semantic quality. Three findings emerge. First, RL significantly improves pragmatic and syntactic quality while preserving semantic fidelity, reducing output variability by more than sixfold. Second, equal reward weighting consistently outperforms targeted weighting: emphasizing a specific dimension fails to improve it and can collapse the model into a low-quality mode. Third, design choices interact with model architecture in non-trivial ways: the invalidity penalty is essential for one model but irrelevant for the other, and SFT initialization is indispensable for one architecture but counterproductive for another. These results demonstrate that reward composition is a primary determinant of optimization outcomes, with effects as large as the decision to apply RL itself. The findings generalize to any structured generation task where quality is assessed along multiple automated dimensions. We release our implementation and experimental code at https://github.com/chlauer99/RL_for_process_modeling.

</details>


### [43] [LLM Agents for Deliberative Collaboration: A Study on Joint Decision Making Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06157)
*Chenxu Wang,Yongkun Yang,Boyuan Du,Shiwei Lin,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测联合决策任务中的审议式大语言模型（LLM）智能体，形式化了审议式协作问题，构建了跨领域的可扩展基准，并系统评估了多个代表性LLM。结果显示复杂审议协作任务仍对最先进LLM构成挑战，即使有外部数学工具辅助，模型仍可能在信息对齐的审议过程或复杂推理决策过程中失败；但诊断分析表明审议过程也能提供反思和纠错机会，有时优于集中式基线。该工作为评估和改进审议式LLM智能体奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类协作中自然通过沟通对齐信息并达成共识，审议在协作中起关键作用。现有研究缺乏对部分可观测、非对称观测下多智能体审议式协作的系统形式化与评估基准，需要建立统一框架来探究LLM在审议式协作中的能力、局限及特性。

Method: 1. 将审议式协作形式化为具有部分和非对称观测的合作联合决策问题。2. 构建跨多个任务设置和领域的可扩展基准，要求智能体通过审议交换信息以达成共享奖励的联合决策。3. 实例化参考脚手架与评估协议，对一系列代表性LLM进行系统评估。4. 引入外部数学工具辅助，并进行诊断分析以揭示审议过程中的反思与纠错效应。

Result: 复杂审议协作任务仍显著挑战最先进LLM。即使提供外部数学工具，模型仍可能在审议过程中无法有效对齐信息，或在决策推理阶段失败。然而，诊断分析发现审议过程能提供反思和错误纠正的机会，在某些情况下性能优于集中式基线。

Conclusion: 本文建立了评估和改进审议式LLM智能体的基础框架，揭示了当前基于LLM的多智能体系统在审议协作中的优势、局限及特性，为未来研究提供了重要洞见。

Abstract: Deliberation plays a crucial role in collaboration; when humans work together, they naturally engage in communication to align information and reach an agreement. In this paper, we investigate deliberative large language model (LLM) agents under partially observable joint decision-making tasks. We formalize deliberative collaboration as a cooperative joint decision problem with partial and asymmetric observations, and introduce a scalable benchmark that instantiates this problem across multiple task settings and domains in which agents must exchange information through deliberation to reach a joint decision with a shared reward. We then instantiate a reference scaffold and evaluation protocol for deliberative agents and conduct a systematic evaluation of a range of representative LLMs. The results reveal that complex deliberative collaboration tasks continue to challenge state-of-the-art language models. Even with the aid of external mathematical tools, language models may fail in either the deliberation process for aligning information or the complex reasoning process for making the decision. On the other hand, diagnostic analysis reveals that the deliberation process may also provide opportunities for reflection and error correction, sometimes improving performance over centralized baselines. Altogether, our work establishes a foundation for evaluating and improving LLM agents in deliberative collaboration and provides insights into the strengths, limitations, and properties of current LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [44] [Spider 2.0-AIFunc: Extending Real-World Text-to-SQL to AI-Native SQL Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06229)
*Tianyang Liu,Canwen Xu,Fangyu Lei,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Jixuan Chen,Tao Yu,Julian McAuley,Zhewei Yao,Yuxiong He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了Spider 2.0-AIFunc基准，包含465个跨越125个真实数据库的验证实例，涵盖Snowflake平台上的六类AI函数，用于评估大模型生成AI原生SQL的能力。实验显示最强专有模型达到67-70%执行准确率，最佳开源模型为58.1%，主要差距源于谓词指定、模式接地和AI函数参数化错误。传统文本到SQL的Agent框架无法有效迁移，极简Agent设置反而持平或优于复杂方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本到SQL基准仅评估传统SQL，无法衡量模型生成包含大模型原生函数（如分类、情感分析、实体抽取等）的AI原生SQL的能力，因此需要构建专门的评测基准。

Method: 基于现有企业级文本到SQL基准，通过基于Agent的流水线将源任务重写为AI原生形式，同步转换目标查询并细化自然语言指令，使预期的AI原生解法显式化并减少歧义。所有实例通过跨时间窗口的多轮重复执行协议验证结果稳定性后发布。

Result: 评测十个最先进语言模型，最强专有模型执行准确率达67-70%，最佳开源模型为58.1%。性能差距主要源于谓词指定、模式接地和AI函数参数化错误。针对传统文本到SQL设计的Agent框架（如模式检索、相关表选择）无法有效迁移到AI原生SQL：极简Agent设置持续匹配或优于更复杂的替代方案。

Conclusion: Spider 2.0-AIFunc填补了AI原生SQL生成评测的空白，揭示了当前模型在AI函数参数化、模式接地等方面的短板，并表明针对传统SQL设计的复杂Agent策略在AI原生SQL场景下并不关键，简单方法反而更有效。

Abstract: Major cloud data platforms now expose large language model capabilities as native SQL functions, enabling analysts to perform classification, filtering, sentiment analysis, extraction, similarity search, and aggregation within ordinary SQL queries. Yet existing text-to-SQL benchmarks evaluate only conventional SQL and provide no signal on whether models can generate such AI-native SQL. We introduce Spider 2.0-AIFunc, a benchmark of 465 verified instances across 125 real-world databases covering six types of AI functions on the Snowflake platform. Starting from an existing enterprise text-to-SQL benchmark, we construct Spider 2.0-AIFunc through an agent-based pipeline that rewrites source tasks into AI-native form, simultaneously transforming target queries and refining natural language instructions to make the intended AI-native solution explicit and reduce ambiguity. All instances pass a multi-round repeated execution protocol across temporally separated windows to confirm result stability before release. Evaluating ten state-of-the-art language models, we find that the strongest proprietary models reach 67-70% execution accuracy while the best open-source model achieves 58.1%, a gap driven primarily by errors in predicate specification, schema grounding, and AI function parameterization. Agent frameworks designed for traditional text-to-SQL challenges, such as schema retrieval and relevant table selection, do not transfer effectively to AI-native SQL: a minimal agent setup consistently matches or outperforms more elaborate alternatives, suggesting that the strategies these frameworks employ are less critical in this setting. Data are available at https://github.com/Leolty/Spider2-AIFunc .

</details>

### [45] [Prompt-to-Paper: Agentic AI System for Bioinformatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05456)
*Ramsha Kamran,Maheera Amjad,Zartasha Mustansar,Arsalan Shaukat,Salma Sherbaz,Muhammad U. S. Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prompt-to-Paper 是一个多智能体框架，解决了大语言模型自动生成论文的三大缺陷：声明缺乏可验证文献支撑、实验结果捏造、缺乏标准化质量评估。它通过确定性检索增强生成、自主编码代理执行真实实验、八维自动质量评分器及质量驱动改进循环，在五个生物信息学案例研究中生成可投稿格式的 PDF，平均质量提升 17.96 分，人工评审均分 7.0/10，成本约 0.31 美元/篇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型自动生成论文系统存在三大关键缺陷：(i) 生成的声明无法确定性地基于可验证文献；(ii) 实验结果常为捏造而非真实执行；(iii) 缺乏标准化的多维度框架评估 AI 生成论文是否达到真实发表所需的质量与严谨性。

Method: 提出 Prompt-to-Paper 多智能体框架，包含三大创新：1) 确定性检索增强生成管道，结合分段感知相关性评分与滚雪球式引文扩展，将每个声明锚定在 60-100 篇可验证论文语料中；2) 自主编码代理执行真实的计算生物学实验，替代合成输出；3) 八维自动化质量评分器，结合已发表论文的近似参考统计与显式幻觉惩罚，提供标准化、可复现的质量评估。质量驱动改进循环利用上下文丰富的修订器，将每次迭代路由至三种研究者动作之一，每十次迭代触发深度研究循环以重新实验并重新撰写。

Result: 在五个生物信息学案例研究中验证：所有五个案例均生成了零引用越界的投稿格式 PDF。改进循环使手稿质量在 0-100 分制上平均提升 17.96 分（最大提升 26.04 分）。作为部分外部检查，人工评审员对五篇手稿的平均打分为 7.0/10。完整手稿生成成本约为 0.31 美元/篇。

Conclusion: Prompt-to-Paper 通过确定性文献 grounding、真实实验执行和标准化多维质量评估，成功填补了 AI 自动生成论文的评估鸿沟，生成的手稿达到可投稿质量，并以极低成本实现了端到端的自动化论文生成流程。

Abstract: While recent advances in large language models have enabled end-to-end automated manuscript generation, existing systems suffer from three critical deficiencies: (i) generated claims are not deterministically grounded in verifiable literature, (ii) experimental results are frequently fabricated rather than executed, and (iii) there exists no standardized, multi-dimensional framework to assess whether AI-generated manuscripts meet the quality and rigor required for real-world publication. We present Prompt-to-Paper, a multi-agent framework that directly addresses this evaluation gap through three integrated innovations. First, a deterministic retrieval-augmented generation pipeline with section-aware relevance scoring and snowball citation expansion grounds every claim in a verifiable corpus of 60--100 papers. Second, an autonomous coding agent executes real computational biology experiments replacing synthetic outputs with genuine numerical results. Third, an eight-dimensional automated quality scorer, benchmarked with approximate reference statistics from published papers and augmented with explicit hallucination penalties, provides standardized, reproducible quality assessments. The quality-driven improvement loop uses a context-rich reviser that routes each iteration to one of three researcher actions and fires a deep research cycle every ten iterations to re-run experiments and re-manuscript from stronger outputs. We validate the system on five bioinformatics case studies; all five cases compiled submission-formatted PDFs with zero out-of-range citations. The improvement loop raises manuscript quality by an average of +17.96 points on a 0--100 scale (maximum +26.04. As partial external checks, a human reviewer scored the five manuscripts at an average of 7.0 out of 10. Complete manuscripts are produced at approximately 0.31 USD per paper.

</details>


### [46] [From Graphs to Gradients: Physics-Inspired Structural Attribution for Cyber-Physical IoT Systems and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05563)
*Spyridon Evangelatos,Christos Diou,Georgios Th. Papadopoulos,Evangelos Markakis,Panagiotis Sarigiannidis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统计力学的无向能量模型框架，用于工业物联网等混合网络物理系统的可解释因果归因，无需恢复有向因果图即可实现依赖感知的归因与扰动推理，实验表明其在归因准确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性上优于基于图的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可解释性方法主要基于输入输出相关性，而因果解释关注干预问题，能提供更稳健的洞察。但在大规模混合网络物理系统中，由于反馈回路和部分可观测性，恢复显式有向因果结构往往不切实际，因此需要一种不依赖有向因果图的依赖感知归因方法。

Method: 受统计力学启发，构建无向能量基础表示来建模变量依赖，通过分析能量景观变化反映个别组件的影响，实现严格的依赖感知归因，并支持跨混合交互的扰动效应推理，无需恢复有向因果图。

Result: 在工业物联网测试床上的仿真实验显示，该框架在归因准确性、鲁棒性和可扩展性方面优于最先进的基于图的方法，能为异常行为提供可靠解释，虽不完全恢复系统生成动力学，但提供有价值的依赖感知解释，支持人类理解和下游预测诊断任务。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维网络物理系统和社会技术系统提供了原理性的结构化解释，虽在工业物联网安全中验证，但也适用于其他需要结构化解释的复杂系统。

Abstract: Interpretable explanation methods in Artificial Intelligence aim to uncover the underlying causes and their effects, enabling a deeper understanding of why a system behaves in a certain way under different inputs. Unlike traditional explainability methods, which mainly highlight correlations between input and output variables, causal explanation focuses on interventional questions. By doing so, it provides more robust insights, helping users understand automated decisions, especially in high-risk domains. Recovering an explicit directed causal structure, however, is often impractical in large-scale, hybrid cyber-physical systems with feedback loops and partial observability. This paper introduces a novel framework inspired by statistical mechanics that instead models variable dependencies through an undirected, energy-based representation of cyber-physical IoT systems. Our approach enables rigorous dependency-aware attribution by analysing how variations in the energy landscape reflect the influence of individual components, without recovering a directed causal graph. It also supports reasoning about perturbation effects across hybrid interactions, providing reliable explanations of abnormal behaviours. We empirically examined our framework through simulations on an industrial IoT testbed with hybrid continuous and discrete variables, demonstrating higher attribution accuracy, improved robustness and better scalability than state-of-the-art graph-based approaches. While the attributions are not intended to fully recover the system's generative dynamics, they provide valuable, dependency-aware explanations supporting both human interpretation and downstream predictive and diagnostic tasks. Although demonstrated in industrial IoT security, our framework also applies to other high-dimensional cyber-physical and socio-technical systems requiring principled, structural explanations.

</details>


### [47] [CSTutorBench: Benchmarking Small Language Models as Tutors for Block-Based Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05571)
*H. Chad Lane,Bryson Kageler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CSTutorBench基准，用于评估语言模型在VEX VR积木编程环境中作为计算机科学导师的表现，发现模型在表层标准（词汇、语气）表现良好，但在深层教学行为（避免答案泄露、结合学生调试历史）上存在不足；模型家族和指令微调方法比参数量更能预测教学质量，基于教育提示工程的针对性提示修订能显著提升多数模型得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为AI导师在K-12教育部署面临隐私、成本和专有模型依赖问题，小语言模型（SLM）虽是有前景的替代方案，但针对特定教育场景（如积木编程）选择合适模型困难，因为该领域数据在模型训练中基本缺失。

Method: 构建CSTutorBench基准，包含17个基于场景的问题，依据建立在辅导与反馈研究基础上的教学评分量表评分，采用人工介入的LLM-as-judge评估流水线。在11个模型（4B-120B参数）上进行初步评测，并基于近期教育提示工程研究进行针对性提示修订实验。

Result: 模型在表层标准（词汇、语气）表现良好，但在深层教学行为（避免答案泄露、结合学生调试历史）上表现不佳。模型家族和指令微调方法似乎比单纯参数量更能预测教学质量（但样本量小限制结论强度）。针对性提示修订使11个模型中10个得分提升。

Conclusion: 研究强调了针对特定教育情境、建立在教学理论基础上的基准测试对SLM在教育部署中选型的重要价值。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly explored as AI tutors, yet deploying them in K-12 settings raises concerns around privacy, cost, and reliance on proprietary models. Small language models (SLMs) offer a promising alternative, but selecting the right model for a specific educational context remains difficult, particularly when the target domain, such as block-based programming, is largely absent from model training data. We introduce CSTutorBench, a benchmark for evaluating language models as CS tutors in VEX VR, a block-based robotics environment. The benchmark comprises 17 scenario-based questions scored against a pedagogical rubric grounded in established tutoring and feedback research, with a human-in-the-loop LLM-as-judge pipeline for evaluation. Preliminary findings across 11 models (4B-120B parameters) reveal that models perform well on surface-level criteria such as vocabulary and tone but struggle with deeper pedagogical behaviors, particularly avoiding answer leakage and engaging with student debugging histories. In our sample, model family and instruction-tuning approach appear to be better predictors of tutoring quality than parameter count alone, though the small number of models limits the strength of this conclusion. A targeted prompt revision grounded in recent educational prompt engineering research improved scores for 10 of 11 models. These results underscore the value of context-specific, pedagogically grounded benchmarks for SLM selection in educational deployment.

</details>


### [48] [Foundation Models for Automatic CAD Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05573)
*J de Curtò,Victoria Guillén,I. de Zarzà*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLMForge框架，通过两种评价机制（IterTracer和IterVision）评估七大基础模型在97个机械零件设计任务上的文本转CAD生成能力，发现紧凑指令微调模型可媲美大模型，VLM语义评价能实现100%水密网格但会在旋转对称几何体上出现评分分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）和视觉语言模型（VLM）的进步，自动从自然语言生成参数化3D设计成为可能，但缺乏统一的评估流程和基准来系统研究基础模型在机械CAD生成中的表现，因此需要实证研究来填补空白并指导工业应用。

Method: 构建LLMForge多模型文本转CAD框架，集成JSON Schema验证、解析特征评分、网格合成和多轮迭代优化；设计两种评价机制：IterTracer基于光线追踪渲染和解析视觉指标（轮廓IoU、孔可见性、边缘间隙、长宽比一致性）提供轻量几何感知反馈；IterVision用VLM语义评论员（Qwen2.5-VL-72B）通过思维链视觉推理评估空间连贯性和设计意图。在包含四类典型几何族（带孔和螺栓圆的板、多特征盒、法兰圆柱、L型支架）的97个工程设计问题基准上，评估七个基础模型。

Result: IterTracer下前四模型整体均分在[0.885,0.890]形成紧密簇，网格成功率98.97%，表明紧凑指令微调模型可匹配更大规模模型。IterVision下领先模型实现100%水密网格生成，但揭示在旋转对称几何（如圆柱）上视觉与语义评分分歧最大，系统性难点显现。

Conclusion: 研究建立了统一的CAD生成评估基准和迭代优化框架，证明了紧凑模型在机械设计任务上的竞争力，同时揭示了VLM语义评价在对称几何上的局限，为工业级自动化机械设计工作流提供了实证依据和改进方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) enable the automatic generation of parametric 3D designs from natural-language specifications. This chapter presents an empirical study of foundation models for automatic Computer-Aided Design (CAD) generation of mechanical parts, using a unified evaluation pipeline and a curated benchmark of 97 engineering design problems. We introduce LLMForge, a multi-model text-to-CAD framework integrating JSON-schema validation, analytic feature scoring, mesh synthesis, and multi-round iterative refinement, studied under two critique regimes. IterTracer uses a Phong-shaded ray-trace renderer with analytic visual metrics (silhouette IoU, hole visibility, edge clearance, aspect-ratio conformance) for lightweight geometry-aware feedback across rounds. IterVision replaces the analytic scorer with a VLM semantic critic (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) that evaluates rendered views via chain-of-thought visual reasoning, assessing spatial coherence and design intent. On a benchmark spanning four canonical geometry families (plates with holes and bolt circles, multi-feature boxes, flanged cylinders, and L-brackets), we evaluate seven foundation models: DeepSeek-V3.2, Qwen3-235B-A22B, Llama-3.3-70B, Gemma-3-27B, GLM-4.5, MiniMax-M2.1, and INTELLECT. Under IterTracer, the four highest-ranked models form a tight cluster (overall mean in [0.885, 0.890]) with 98.97% mesh success, showing that compact instruction-tuned models can match substantially larger systems. VLM-based critique in IterVision yields 100% watertight mesh generation on the leading model while surfacing systematic difficulty on rotationally symmetric geometries such as cylinders, where visual and semantic scoring diverge most. We discuss benchmark design, failure modes, CAD-oriented prompting, and implications for industrial workflows and scalable automated mechanical design.

</details>


### [49] [Narrative World Model: Narratology-Grounded Writer Memory for Long-Form Fiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05577)
*Mohammad Saifullah,Thomas Kornmaier,Taaha Kazi,Vasu Sharma,Aditya Sanjiv Kanade,Aanand Kumar Yadav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NWM是一个基于叙事学的作家记忆系统，通过类型化时序状态图和查询条件混合检索，在多跳叙事问答上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇小说作家需要记忆系统来回答关于不断演变的故事状态的多跳问题（谁知道秘密及何时知晓、事件先后顺序、铺垫是否兑现、关系如何转变）。现有通用检索和智能体记忆系统只表示实体和事实，缺乏这些问题所依赖的叙事学结构，导致检索错误证据或无证据。

Method: 提出Narrative World Model (NWM)，结合叙事学基础的类型化时序状态图与查询条件混合检索。使用固定的Opus 4.8阅读器，在可复现的公开语料库和经过验证的多跳基准上，仅使用各系统的章节安全证据进行评估，对比最强的时序知识图谱智能体记忆框架Graphiti/Zep。

Result: NWM在两个语料库的多跳叙事问答上显著优于基线，远超GraphRAG和扁平检索。优势来自表征而非提取伪影：即使用NWM的提取器重建基线，优势依然存在，归因于叙事学基础的结构和查询条件检索，而非图大小或提取器质量。

Conclusion: NWM通过引入叙事学结构和查询条件检索，有效解决了长篇小说作家记忆系统的核心需求，证明了叙事学建模对多跳叙事问答的关键作用。

Abstract: Long-form fiction writers need memory that answers multi-hop questions about evolving story state: who knows a secret and when they learned it, whether an event preceded the narration that revealed it, whether a setup paid off, and how a relationship shifted. General-purpose retrieval and agent-memory systems represent entities and facts but not the narratological structure these questions turn on, so they surface the wrong evidence or none at all. We introduce the Narrative World Model (NWM), a writer-memory system that pairs a narratology-grounded typed temporal-state graph with query-conditioned hybrid retrieval. To measure memory rather than the answerer, we read every system through a single held-constant Opus 4.8 reader over only that system's chapter-safe evidence, on a reproducible public corpus and a validated multi-hop benchmark, and we compare against the strongest existing temporal-knowledge-graph agent-memory framework, Graphiti/Zep (Rasmussen et al., 2025). NWM substantially and significantly outperforms this baseline on multi-hop narratological QA across both corpora, and far exceeds GraphRAG and flat retrieval. The advantage is representational rather than an artifact of extraction: it survives rebuilding the baseline with NWM's own extractor, and traces to its narratology-grounded structure and query-conditioned retrieval, not to graph size or extractor quality.

</details>


### [50] [FirstResearch: Auditable Question Formation for LLM Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05682)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 FirstResearch 框架，通过结构化的 Research Question Certificate（研究问题证书）使 LLM 生成的首个科研问题可审计、可证伪，在十个主题上的 LLM 盲评中显著优于现有基线，消融实验证实证书是核心贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 科研代理虽能协助构思、综述、规划实验，但给出的首个研究问题往往听起来合理却缺乏可供科学家检查的机制、证伪条件或假设，难以审计，亟需一种显式的、基于第一性原理的提问生成机制。

Method: 设计 FirstResearch 框架，核心产物为 Research Question Certificate，包含原始定义、假设、机制模型、张力/矛盾、可证伪假设、最小决定性测试、失败更新规则七大字段；在十个 LLM 代理研究主题上对比 AI co-scientist、Agent Laboratory、AI Scientist-v2 等基线，采用 DeepSeek 盲评为主、Gemini-2.5-Flash 复评为辅，并做单次消融检查点实验。

Result: DeepSeek 盲评中 FirstResearch 得 4.86/5，最强基线 4.38/5；Gemini 复评保持排名，平均皮尔逊相关系数 0.865。仅保留证书组件得分分别达 4.90/5 和 4.88/5；移除证书后两评分均跌破 1/5，证实证书为最强组件。

Conclusion: 尽管结果基于 LLM 评判而非人类专家，仍初步支持显式推导约束是让 LLM 生成科研问题更可审计的有效机制，代码与复现脚本已开源。

Abstract: LLM systems for scientific discovery increasingly assist with ideation, literature synthesis, experiment planning, and report generation, but the first research question they propose can remain difficult to audit: it may sound plausible without exposing the mechanism, falsifier, or assumption that a scientist should inspect. We introduce FirstResearch, a first-principles research-question formation framework for scientific LLM agents whose core artifact is a structured Research Question Certificate. The certificate records primitive definitions, assumptions, a mechanism model, a tension or contradiction, a falsifiable hypothesis, a minimal decisive test, and a failure update rule, making the proposed question inspectable before downstream execution. On ten LLM-agent research topics, FirstResearch outperforms controlled prompt-level baselines inspired by AI co-scientist, Agent Laboratory, and AI Scientist-v2 under a primary DeepSeek-blind-judge protocol. A Gemini-2.5-Flash independent-judge rescore of the same 40 baseline packages preserves the system-level ranking, with FirstResearch scoring 4.86/5 versus 4.38/5 for the strongest baseline and Pearson agreement of 0.865 on average score. A one-repeat ablation checkpoint further suggests that the certificate-centered core is the strongest component: certificate-only scoring reaches 4.90/5 under DeepSeek and 4.88/5 under Gemini, while removing certificates drops below 1/5 under both judges. These results are preliminary and use LLM judges rather than human domain experts, but they support a narrow scientific-discovery claim: explicit derivation constraints are a promising mechanism for making LLM-generated scientific questions more auditable. Code, prompts, saved outputs, and reproduction scripts are available at https://github.com/louiswang524/FirstResearch.

</details>


### [51] [Memory in the Loop: In-Process Retrieval as ExtendedWorking Memory for Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05690)
*Yusuf Khan,Carlo Lipizzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究将记忆存储移入语言智能体循环内部（每步读写）的可行性。核心发现：网络存储的高延迟（数十至数百毫秒）是主要障碍，而进程内存储仅需~100微秒，将每步开销降低三个数量级。实验表明：延迟直接导致冗余动作单调上升（进程内0/12 vs 云端110ms时7.2/12）；四款GPT-5级模型在有界窗口下召回率从0/5提升至3.6-4.8/5；存储零丢失（244/244写入保留），缺失全因智能体读取策略。瓶颈实为嵌入计算（网络端200-400ms），本地小模型嵌入+进程内存储可将全链路压缩至~40微秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言智能体将记忆置于循环外部，仅每轮查询一次。受限于网络存储的高延迟（数十至数百毫秒），将记忆纳入循环会使端到端延迟膨胀高达83倍。先前工作仅通过调度或限制检索频次来管理成本，而未质疑延迟本质。论文主张：延迟是存储部署位置的属性，而非循环内模式的必然；若存储足够快（进程内~100us），依据延伸心智论的平等原则，它将成为延伸工作记忆而非单纯咨询工具。

Method: 采用因果实验设计：固定每轮记忆延迟预算，仅变换存储响应速度（进程内 vs 云端110ms往返），测量冗余动作数量（12项任务）。端到端演示：四款GPT-5级模型、有界上下文窗口、进程内向量存储（p50 80-165us）、本地小模型嵌入器。对比基线：显式重述每轮回复策略。指标：召回率（5项事实）、存储写入保真度（244次写入）、每步延迟分解。统计检验：精确排列检验 p=0.0079。

Result: 1) 延迟因果效应：进程内速度冗余动作0.0/12，云端110ms达7.2/12（p=0.0079）。2) 召回提升：四模型均从0/5升至3.6-4.8/5。3) 存储零丢失：244/244写入保留；所有缺失归因于智能体读取策略而非存储。4) 瓶颈重定位：主导成本为嵌入（网络200-400ms）；本地嵌入+进程内存储将完整操作压缩至~40us。5) 基线对比：重述策略完美解决召回，但Token成本随工作集增长。

Conclusion: 记忆延迟是部署位置属性而非架构必然。进程内存储（~100us）使循环内读写成为现实，将外部存储转化为延伸工作记忆，显著减少冗余动作并提升召回。真正瓶颈在于嵌入计算而非存储本身；本地轻量嵌入器配合进程内存储可实现~40us全链路延迟，为高频记忆交互的智能体系统提供了可行工程路径。

Abstract: Language agents run a loop - observe, reason, act - but the memory they reason over sits outside it: a store queried at most once per turn. We study the regime where memory moves inside the loop, read and written on every step. The obstacle has always been latency: networked stores answer in tens to hundreds of milliseconds, and in-loop retrieval can inflate end-to-end latency by up to 83x when retrieval is expensive. Prior work manages that cost rather than questioning it: serving-layer scheduling hides it, "memory-first" designs ration retrieval to once per turn. We argue latency is a property of where the store lives, not the in-loop pattern: an in-process store answers in ~100us, three orders of magnitude below the network regime, and at that speed the per-step tax collapses. By the extended-mind thesis's parity principle, a store fast enough to be constantly and directly available becomes extended working memory, not a tool the agent merely consults. The premise is causal: holding a fixed per-turn memory-latency budget and varying only the store's answer speed, redundant actions rise monotonically with latency - 0.0 of 12 at in-process speed, 7.2 of 12 at a 110ms cloud round trip (gpt-5-nano, gpt-5-mini; exact permutation p=0.0079). We demonstrate the regime end-to-end: across four GPT-5-class models under a bounded window, recall improves from 0/5 to 3.6-4.8/5 with in-loop memory, store ops at p50 80-165us - though an instructed restate-every-reply baseline also solves it perfectly, at a token cost that grows with the working set. The store never lost a fact in any run (244 of 244 writes kept); every miss traces to the agent's read policy, not the store. Our measurements also relocate the bottleneck: the dominant per-step cost is embedding (~200-400ms over the network); pairing the in-process store with a small local embedder returns the complete operation to a measured ~40us.

</details>

*Yang Liu,Zhaokai Luo,Huayi Jin,Ruozhou He,Chenchen Hong,Zhiyong Wang,Yifei Liu,Yunfei Gu,Chentao Wu,Junhao Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Akashic 低开销记忆系统，基于 MemAttention 将上下文组织为有界块并建模跨块语义关系，结合软硬件协同内存放置，显著提升 LLM 代理系统的任务准确性、吞吐率和可持续请求率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理系统在多轮交互、工具调用和跨会话工作流中持续积累上下文，重放完整历史导致预填开销大、可能超出上下文限制，且任务相关证据被无关内容掩埋，降低服务效率和输出质量。

Method: 设计 Akashic 记忆系统，核心为 MemAttention：将上下文划分为有界块，建模块间语义关系以保留跨块证据，避免重复重写完整历史；采用软硬件协同内存放置策略，将可能共同检索的块放置在一起，减少检索碎片化和 I/O 开销。

Result: 在四种代表性负载和三种模型规模上，Akashic 相比强基线将任务准确性提升高达 10.2 个百分点，吞吐率提升高达 1.21 倍，可持续请求率提升高达 1.88 倍。

Conclusion: Akashic 通过基于块的语义建模和软硬件协同内存管理，有效解决了长上下文重放的效率与质量难题，为 LLM 代理系统提供了可扩展、高性能的记忆基础设施。

Abstract: Recent LLM-based agent systems continuously accumulate context across multi-turn interactions, tool invocations, and cross-session workflows. Replaying the full history for every request quickly becomes impractical: long contexts increase prefill cost, may exceed context limits, and often bury task-relevant evidence in irrelevant content, degrading both serving efficiency and output quality. We propose Akashic, a low-overhead memory system built around MemAttention, which organizes context into bounded chunks and models semantic relationships across chunks, preserving cross-chunk evidence without repeatedly rewriting the full history. Akashic further applies hardware-software co-designed memory placement to co-locate likely co-retrieved chunks, reducing retrieval fragmentation and I/O overhead. Across four representative workloads and three model sizes, Akashic improves task accuracy by up to 10.2 points, throughput by up to 1.21x, and sustainable request rate by up to 1.88x over strong prior memory baselines.

</details>

*Yunhan Xu,Qifeng Wu,Xunjin Li,Yuanwei Bin,Qingsong Yao,Jianghang Gu,Guan Wang,Weihao Lv,Huiyu Yang,Wenfa Luo,Jiao Xiang,Yuntian Chen,Shiyi Chen*

TL;DR: 本文提出CAD-IR（CAD中间表示）和基于技能的工业CAD智能体，通过专家知识蒸馏将CATIA操作记录转化为可复用参数化技能，解决文本到CAD生成中提示词模糊、缺乏专业程序知识的问题，实现可编辑的生产级B-Rep模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到CAD方法在处理模糊、欠规约或仅描述高层设计意图的用户提示时表现不佳，且未充分利用工业工作流中自然存在的专家程序知识（如CATIA操作记录、宏日志、工程说明等）。需要一种能连接模糊文本意图与可执行CAD构建的表示和智能体框架。

Method: 核心是CAD-IR：一种可执行的过程化表示，编码参数、有序操作、MCP工具绑定、依赖关系、生成实体和验证规则。CAD-IR双重作用：1）作为载体将专家CAD程序蒸馏为可复用参数化技能；2）为模糊或中间层级提示提供过程化脚手架，转化为完整可执行CAD操作。智能体流程：检索专家技能 → 实例化并修订CAD-IR → 通过CATIA-MCP后端执行 → 多视角视觉反馈迭代优化 → 生成生产级B-Rep模型。

Result: 在Text2CAD基准上，CAD-IR将中间提示的平均Chamfer Distance从14.83降至9.88，显著提升生成质量。在四个复杂汽车零部件上，CAD-IR成功将专家CATIA录制蒸馏为可复用技能，使智能体能为新变体需求生成可编辑的CATIA原生B-Rep模型。

Conclusion: CAD-IR作为专家知识蒸馏载体和程序化脚手架，有效弥合了模糊文本意图与可执行CAD构建的鸿沟，结合技能检索、MCP执行后端和视觉反馈闭环，实现了工业级可编辑参数化B-Rep模型的自动化生成，为工业CAD自动化提供了可落地的技术路径。

Abstract: Computer-aided design (CAD) for industrial components requires long-horizon procedural modeling, robust feature dependencies, editable parametric geometry, and production-grade B-Rep execution. Existing text-to-CAD methods have made promising progress in generating CAD programs from natural-language descriptions, but they still struggle when user prompts are ambiguous, underspecified, or only describe high-level design intent. They also rarely exploit expert procedural knowledge naturally available in industrial workflows, such as CATIA operation recordings, macro logs, drawing notes, and engineering descriptions. We present \algname, a skill-guided industrial CAD agent with expert-grounded knowledge distillation. The core of \algname is CAD intermediate representation (CAD-IR), an executable procedural representation that encodes parameters, ordered operations, MCP tool bindings, dependencies, generated entities, and verification rules. CAD-IR plays two key roles: it first serves as the carrier for distilling expert CAD procedures into reusable parameterized skills; then it provides a procedural scaffold that turns vague or intermediate-level prompts into complete executable CAD operations. \algname retrieves expert-derived skills, instantiates and revises CAD-IR, executes the resulting procedure through a dedicated CATIA-MCP backend, and uses multi-view visual feedback for iterative refinement, and finally generates production-ready B-Rep models. On the Text2CAD benchmark, CAD-IR improves generation from intermediate prompts by reducing mean Chamfer Distance from $14.83$ to $9.88$, showing its ability to bridge ambiguous textual intent and executable CAD construction. On four complex automotive components, CAD-IR enables expert CATIA recordings to be distilled into reusable skills, allowing \algname to generate editable CATIA-native B-Rep models for new variant requests.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据驱动营销依赖大量消费者数据，但收集成本高、耗时且难以规模化。研究旨在验证LLM是否可作为低成本、可扩展的替代方案，为投射技术（挖掘消费者联想、情感、需求的方法）生成合成数据。

Method: 研究设计：选取城市旅游目的地感知为案例，收集人类原始研究数据作为基准。系统测试多个LLM、多种提示策略和温度设置，在多个投射任务上生成响应。分析维度包括语言度量、多样性与集中度指标、主题模型和高频词分析，全方位对比人类与LLM响应的异同。

Result: LLM生成响应与人类响应在宏观主题和联想上呈现显著重叠，表明LLM能捕捉核心消费者洞察。但两者在表达风格、语言结构复杂度、多样性来源（如LLM倾向于语义变异而人类源于个体差异）上存在系统性差异。不同模型、提示和温度设置会显著影响合成数据的质量和特征。

Conclusion: LLM可作为生成合成消费者数据的有力工具，尤其适用于探索性研究和早期洞察挖掘，能大幅降低数据收集成本。但需谨慎选择模型、设计提示并设定温度，且必须认识到LLM无法完全复刻人类响应的细微差异与真实多样性，合成数据应作为补充而非完全替代真实消费者研究。

Abstract: Modern data-driven marketing relies on large amounts of consumer data, yet collecting such data can be costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. This research examines whether large language models (LLMs) can be used to generate synthetic consumer data for projective techniques, a set of methods designed to elicit consumer associations, emotions, wants, and needs. We test LLM-generated responses across multiple projective tasks, LLMs, prompting strategies, and temperature settings, and compare them with human responses from a primary research study on perceptions of city tourism destinations. Human and LLM responses were analyzed using linguistic measures, diversity and concentration metrics, topic models, and top-term analyses. The results show substantial overlap between human and LLM responses in broad topics and associations, but also important differences in style, linguistic structure, and the way diversity is generated. Recommendations are given on how to best utilize LLMs for generating synthetic consumer data, how model and prompt choices shape response quality, and on recognizing the limitations of LLM synthetic consumer data generation.

</details>


### [55] [Beyond Static Evaluation: Building Simulation Environments for Scalable Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05773)
*Akshay Arora,Ishan Nigam,Ashutosh Aggarwal,Shefali Bansal,Krishna Singh,Sweta Kumari,Nikhil Mittal,Shariq Farhan,Siddarth Malreddy*

Main category: cs.AI

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建 AgenticAI-Supervisor 平台：1) 采用 API 和 UI 驱动的 RL Gym 环境，将环境创建与可扩展执行解耦；2) 基于可验证的执行结果生成高保真轨迹；3) 应用多维奖励塑造机制；4) 通过严格的内部状态验证和测试来缓解奖励作弊；5) 通过客服代理案例研究验证平台的闭环反馈能力。

Result: 在客服代理案例研究中，平台展示了稳定的一致闭环反馈，能够有效支持模型优化。验证了核心功能：环境解耦、高保真轨迹生成、多维奖励塑造、奖励作弊缓解等能力。

Conclusion: AgenticAI-Supervisor 为自主代理评估提供了一个基础性平台，实现了可验证执行结果驱动的评估、多维奖励塑造和奖励作弊缓解。未来将扩展 Computer Use、Tool Use、自动化难题生成和边缘情况生成等高级功能，进一步增强代理评估与优化能力。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) evolve into autonomous agents, traditional static evaluation fails to capture multi-step decision-making. We introduce AgenticAI-Supervisor, an API and UI-driven RL Gym environment that decouples environment creation from scalable execution. By moving to verifiable execution outcomes, the platform generates high-fidelity traces and applies multi-dimensional reward shaping. Critically, our framework mitigates reward hacking through rigorous internal state validation and testing. This work provides a first look at our platform's core capabilities through a Customer Support Agent case study demonstrating a consistent closed-loop feedback for model optimization. Future work will focus on advanced features such as Computer Use, Tool Use, automated "stumping", and edge-case generation.

</details>


### [56] [Beyond the Leaderboard: A Synthesis of Tool-Use, Planning, and Reasoning Failures in Large Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05775)
*Wael Albayaydh,Rui Zhao,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文综合了27篇关于LLM代理的基准、分类和审计论文，构建了首个统一的LLM代理局限性分类体系，识别出六大失败簇，并发现失败随任务长度非线性累积，子任务成功难以保证端到端成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准报告的性能提升往往掩盖了跨不同评估工作中反复出现的失败模式，缺乏一个整合工具使用、规划、长时程推理、多代理协作、安全性和测量有效性的统一分类框架。

Method: 通过迭代方式综合27篇论文（2023-2026年，涵盖19个不同基准），将独立报告的错误类别按代理推理到行动流水线的不同阶段分组为主题。

Result: 识别出六大失败簇：(1)工具调用与参数级错误，(2)规划与约束满足失败，(3)上下文累积导致的长时程退化，(4)多代理协作失败，(5)对抗或欠规约条件下的安全失败，(6)测量有效性问题。发现失败随任务长度非线性累积，子任务强表现不保证端到端成功，额外脚手架不一定提升可靠性；单轮工具使用、短时程网页导航和窄域编码任务已取得显著进展。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了首个跨维度整合的LLM代理局限性统一分类，揭示了代理失败的系统性模式，为未来研究和评估提供了结构化框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly evaluated on their ability to use tools, plan multi-step tasks, coordinate with other agents, and operate over extended horizons. Reported benchmark gains often obscure recurring failure modes documented across otherwise unrelated evaluation efforts. This paper synthesizes 27 benchmark, taxonomy, and audit papers (2023-2026), spanning 19 distinct benchmarks, into a cross-cutting taxonomy of agent limitations. To our knowledge, this is the first synthesis that integrates evidence across tool use, planning, long-horizon reasoning, multi-agent coordination, safety, and measurement validity into a single, unified taxonomy of LLM agent limitations. We identify six failure clusters: (1) tool invocation and parameter-level errors, (2) planning and constraint-satisfaction failures, (3) long-horizon degradation from context accumulation, (4) multi-agent coordination failures, (5) safety and security failures under adversarial or underspecified conditions, and (6) measurement validity problems. The taxonomy was derived iteratively by grouping independently reported error categories into themes corresponding to distinct stages of the agent reasoning-to-action pipeline. Across the literature, we find that failures compound nonlinearly with task length, that strong performance on individual sub-tasks does not reliably translate into end-to-end success, and that additional scaffolding does not consistently improve reliability. At the same time, substantial progress has been demonstrated in single-turn tool use, short-horizon web navigation, and narrowly scoped coding tasks.

</details>


### [57] [Controlling Tool Use with Heading-Specific Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05790)
*Yuqi Chen,Vincent Siu,Yang Liu,Dawn Song,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究工具增强大语言模型中的工具使用决策，发现从标题锚点位置提取的转向向量可跨五个开源模型和三个领域双向因果控制工具调用行为，在参数化推理足够的领域最有效抑制不必要的工具使用；但几何分析显示工具调用步骤呈现弥散双峰对齐而非线性编码预测的一致负对齐，不同工具类型招募截然不同的内部特征，揭示工具的非参数化本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强大语言模型倾向于不必要地调用外部工具。由于工具仅存在于推理时的上下文中，在模型权重中无直接编码，因此工具使用决策是否存在可提取和操纵的稳定内部表征是一个非平凡问题。

Method: 从标题锚点位置提取转向向量，在五个开源模型和三个领域测试其对工具调用行为的双向因果控制效果；同时进行几何分析，考察工具调用步骤与抑制向量的对齐模式及不同工具类型间的特征重叠情况。

Result: 转向向量在参数化推理足够的领域最有效抑制不必要的工具使用。然而几何分析揭示：工具调用步骤与抑制向量呈现弥散、双峰对齐，而非线性编码预测的一致负对齐；不同工具类型招募截然不同的内部特征，跨工具特征重叠度低。

Conclusion: 这些几何特性表明工具的非参数化本质，将工具使用转向向量与参数化概念的转向向量区分开来。这种几何不规则性与观察到的因果有效性之间的关系仍是一个开放问题。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language models extend their capabilities beyond parametric knowledge through external tools, but tend to invoke them unnecessarily. We investigate whether tool-use decisions have any stable internal representation that can be extracted and manipulated, a question that is non-trivial given that tools exist entirely in context at inference time and have no direct encoding in model weights. We show that steering vectors extracted from heading-anchors positions exert bidirectional causal control over tool-invocation behavior across five open-source models and three domains, suppressing unnecessary tool use most effectively in domains where parametric reasoning suffices. However, geometric analysis reveals that this causal effectiveness does not correspond to clean linear structure: tool-invocation steps exhibit diffuse, bimodal alignment with the suppression vector rather than the consistent negative alignment a linear encoding account would predict, and different tool types recruit largely distinct internal signatures with low cross-tool feature overlap. We hypothesize these geometric properties are indicative of the non-parametric nature of tools, and distinguish tool-use steering vectors from those extracted for parametrically grounded concepts. The relationship between this geometric irregularity and the observed causal effectiveness remains an open question.

</details>

*Yue Xu,Yutao Sun,Yihao Liu,Mengyu Zhou,Jiayi Qiao,Lu Ma,Kai Tang,Wenjie Wang,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NapMem是一个长期用户记忆框架，将用户历史组织为多粒度记忆金字塔，并通过记忆工具暴露给智能体，采用强化学习训练智能体主动选择记忆粒度，在多个记忆密集型任务上表现优异，同时保留通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统多采用被动检索接口，导致模型只能消费预选证据，无法根据查询动态选择合适粒度的记忆，限制了个性化对话代理的长期记忆利用效果。

Method: 提出NapMem框架：将用户历史构建为链接的多粒度记忆金字塔（原始对话、类型化记忆记录、主题轨迹、用户画像），通过溯源关系连接；暴露记忆工具供智能体调用；采用记忆工具强化学习训练智能体根据查询和中间证据主动选择记忆粒度。

Result: 在PersonaMem-v2、LongMemEval和LoCoMo三个基准上，NapMem智能体在多样化记忆密集型任务中表现具有竞争力；非记忆任务评估显示学习到的策略基本保留了通用推理和工具使用能力；额外分析了存储、推理成本、工具使用行为及导航、记忆粒度、RL训练的消融实验。

Conclusion: 长期用户记忆通过结构化存储与学习到的记忆使用策略相结合，能够在适当粒度上有效利用记忆，提升个性化对话代理的性能。

Abstract: Long-term user memory is essential for personalized conversational agents, yet many memory systems still expose memory through passive retrieval interfaces, making the model a consumer of pre-selected evidence. We introduce NapMem, a framework for learning to use long-term user memory as a structured action space rather than passively retrieved context. NapMem organizes user history into a linked multi-granularity memory pyramid, where raw conversations, typed memory records, topic tracks, and user profiles are connected through provenance relations, and exposes these levels through memory tools. The agent is trained to select memory according to the query and intermediate evidence, allowing it to inspect different memory granularities before answering. Experiments on PersonaMem-v2, LongMemEval, and LoCoMo show that a NapMem agent trained with memory-tool reinforcement learning is competitive across diverse memory-intensive tasks, while evaluations on non-memory tasks suggest that the learned policy largely preserves general reasoning and tool-use abilities. Additional analyses examine storage, inference cost, tool-use behavior, and ablations over navigation, memory granularity, and RL training. Our results suggest that long-term user memory benefits from coupling structured storage with a learned policy for using memory at the appropriate granularity.

</details>


### [59] [TurnOPD: Making On-Policy Distillation Turn-Aware for Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05804)
*Yuhang Zhou,Kai Zheng,Haoling Li,Dengyun Peng,Can Xu,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长时程智能体任务中标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的两大低效问题——全轨迹采样浪费算力于尾部回合的弱监督、轨迹级KL损失集中于浅层token导致深层决策回合欠训练——本文提出TurnOPD，包含自适应回合深度预算（基于探测统计决定回滚长度）与渐进式回合归一化损失预算（将KL权重从token级逐步转向回合平衡监督），在ALFWorld、WebShop、Multi-Hop Search三个基准上显著提升等算力下的验证准确率并推进准确率-时间帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线策略蒸馏（OPD）虽在语言智能体训练中表现优异，但其在长时程智能体任务中的应用尚未充分探索。研究发现标准OPD存在两大关键低效：1）全时程回滚在尾部回合上浪费大量墙钟时间，而这些回合提供的KL监督信号微弱且噪声大；2）轨迹级KL目标使损失过度集中在前几个token（浅层行为），一旦初始行为对齐，后续深层决策回合便得不到充分训练。这些问题严重制约了OPD在长时程任务上的样本效率与时间效率。

Method: 提出TurnOPD，一种面向长时程智能体的回合级预算策略，包含两个协同的预算控制器：① 自适应回合深度预算：利用轻量探测运行收集的回合统计量（如回合奖励方差、KL散度分布），动态决定每次回滚的最优深度，避免在低信息增益的尾部回合上浪费算力；② 渐进式回合归一化损失预算：将KL损失从token级均匀加权逐步退火为回合级均衡加权，确保训练后期深层决策回合获得与浅层回合相当的梯度信号，实现全时程的均衡监督。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop、Multi-Hop Search三个长时程基准上，使用任务专用教师模型进行实验。在相同墙钟训练预算下，TurnOPD验证准确率全面优于标准OPD，并显著推进了准确率-时间帕累托前沿；消融实验证实两个预算控制器均为性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: TurnOPD通过回合级算力与损失预算的双重控制，有效解决了长时程智能体在线蒸馏中的采样冗余与监督失衡问题，为资源受限下的长时程语言智能体训练提供了更高效的范式，具有广泛的推广潜力。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student policy by matching a stronger teacher on the student's own trajectories, offering a promising framework for language agent training. However, its application to long-horizon agentic tasks remains insufficiently explored. We identify two key inefficiencies in vanilla agent OPD: (1) full-horizon rollouts often waste wall-clock resources on tail turns that provide weak and noisy KL supervision, and (2) trajectory-level KL objectives concentrate most of the loss on shallow tokens, leaving deeper decision turns under-trained once initial behaviors are aligned. To address these challenges, we propose TurnOPD, a turn-level budgeting strategy for efficient on-policy distillation of long-horizon agents. TurnOPD consists of two budget controllers: adaptive rollout-depth budgeting, which uses probe-based turn statistics to determine rollout length, and progressive turn-normalized loss budgeting, which gradually shifts KL weighting from token-level to turn-balanced supervision. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Multi-Hop Search with task-specialized teacher models show that TurnOPD achieves superior validation accuracy under equal wall-clock training budgets and advances the accuracy--time frontier beyond vanilla OPD.

</details>


### [60] [Onnes: A Physics-Grounded Multi-Agent LLM Simulator for Cryogenic Fault Diagnosis in Quantum Computing Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05805)
*Praneeth Narisetty,Uday Kumar Reddy Kattamanchi,Shiva Nagendra Babu Kore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Onnes是一个基于物理的稀释制冷机数字孪生模拟器，结合前向物理模型和学习到的真实冰箱噪声指纹，驱动多智能体LLM运维层，用于低温故障诊断。零样本LLM智能体面板在检测上与监督分类器无显著差异，但在分类上落后；通过精选对比少样本演示和自我一致性投票，分类准确率从0.685提升至0.990，匹配监督分类器（0.985），无需参数更新。连续监测中能在一个轮询间隔内捕获所有发展中的故障，置信度门控抑制了前发作误报。真实硬件测试显示误报率6.4%，物理故障召回率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀释制冷机是超导量子计算机的关键基础设施，但其故障诊断仍主要依赖阈值告警，只能报告“有异常”而无法指出“是什么异常”。需要一种能够精确诊断故障类型的方法。

Method: 构建Onnes数字孪生：耦合真实稀释制冷地板、从BlueFors日志学习的噪声相关性指纹、六个基于物理的故障类别（三个在温度上重叠但在流量和压力上可分离）。使用该孪生驱动多智能体LLM运维层，进行零样本LLM智能体面板与监督ML分类器的对比评估（1000轮）。随后引入精选对比少样本演示和自我一致性投票提升分类性能。在连续监测模式下进行九轮故障种子扫描，并通过置信度门控抑制误报。最后在真实BlueFors遥测数据上训练检测器，进行仿真到真实的验证。

Result: 零样本面板检测性能与监督分类器无显著差异，但分类准确率较低（0.685 vs 0.985），错误集中在易混淆故障。少样本演示+自我一致性投票将分类准确率提升至0.990，匹配监督分类器，消融实验显示增益主要来自演示。连续监测中每个发展故障均在一个轮询间隔内被捕获，置信度门控有效抑制前发作误报（误报率依赖后端）。真实硬件测试：误报率6.4%，物理故障召回率100%。

Conclusion: Onnes展示了基于物理的数字孪生与LLM智能体结合在低温故障诊断中的有效性，少样本演示能显著提升LLM分类性能，达到监督学习水平且无需参数更新。系统具备实时监测和低误报能力，且在真实硬件上验证了泛化性能。

Abstract: Dilution refrigerators are the enabling infrastructure of superconducting quantum computers, yet their fault diagnosis is still dominated by threshold alarms that report that something is wrong, not what. We present Onnes, a physics-grounded digital-twin simulator of a dilution refrigerator (a forward physics model with a learned real-fridge noise fingerprint) that drives a live multi-agent LLM operations layer, and use it for a controlled head-to-head between a zero-shot LLM agent panel and a supervised ML classifier on cryogenic fault diagnosis. The twin couples a real dilution-cooling floor, a noise-and-correlation fingerprint learned from real BlueFors logs, and six physics-grounded fault classes, three engineered to overlap on temperature but separate on flow and pressure. Across a 1000-turn evaluation the zero-shot panel shows no significant difference from the classifier on detection but trails on classification, its errors concentrating on the confusable faults. Curated contrastive few-shot demonstrations and self-consistency voting then raise classification accuracy from 0.685 to 0.990, matching the supervised classifier (0.985) with no parameter updates and six labeled demonstrations; an ablation attributes the gain almost entirely to the demonstrations. Run as a continuous monitor across a nine-run fault-by-seed sweep, the agent catches every developing fault within one poll interval, and a confidence gate suppresses pre-onset false alarms whose rate is backend-dependent. As a first sim-to-real check, a detector trained purely on real BlueFors telemetry posts a real-hardware false-alarm rate of 6.4% and 100% recall on physics faults injected onto real held-out windows. All numbers are drawn verbatim from released run logs.

</details>


### [61] [StateFuse: Deterministic Conflict-Preserving Memory for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05844)
*Sergey Volkov,Yang Li,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: StateFuse 是一种冲突感知的复制内存契约，通过保留冲突而非提前折叠，实现更安全的弃权与可审计修正，但并不带来通用的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统在分支、重试和副本中会积累相互冲突的观测，但许多实际内存层仍采用难以检查或修正的覆盖规则来折叠分歧。

Method: 基于标准 OpSet/CRDT 合并构建 StateFuse，定义面向智能体的语义层：不可变历史、显式冲突对象、精确与语义修正句柄、确定性谓词契约、投影时解析且不可重写复制状态。

Result: 在 282 道官方冲突题 MemoryAgentBench 切片上，对比方法答案准确率持平，但保留冲突的表面能让矛盾可见，折叠表面则不能；受控智能体循环中，保留歧义支持更安全的弃权与修正；消融实验显示当精确前序标识不可用时语义句柄至关重要。

Conclusion: StateFuse 最适合作为面向矛盾显现、弃权和可审计修正的更安全公共内存契约，而非通用准确率增益手段。

Abstract: Agent systems accumulate conflicting observations across branches, retries, and replicas, yet many practical memory layers still collapse disagreement behind overwrite rules that are difficult to inspect or correct. We present StateFuse, a conflict-aware replicated memory contract built on standard OpSet/CRDT merge. StateFuse does not introduce a new join algebra; it defines an agent-facing semantics layer with immutable history, explicit conflict objects, exact and semantic correction handles (claim_id / claim_ref), deterministic predicate contracts, and projection-time resolution that cannot rewrite replicated state.
  We evaluate StateFuse against flat multi-value, raw-log, provenance-style, and collapsed baselines under matched resolver and verification policies. On a 282-question official conflict-bearing MemoryAgentBench slice, the compared methods tie on answer accuracy, but conflict-preserving surfaces keep contradictions visible while collapsed surfaces do not. In a controlled agent loop with uniform verification, preserving ambiguity enables safer abstention and correction than early collapse. A correction-handle ablation further shows that semantic handles matter when exact prior identifiers are unavailable.
  The resulting claim is narrow: StateFuse is best supported as a safer public memory contract for contradiction surfacing, abstention, and auditable correction, not as a universal accuracy gain.

</details>


### [62] [Uncovering Latent Depression Severity for Binary Depression Detection via Advantage-weighting Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05901)
*Manning Gao,Tingyi Liu,Leheng Zhang,Haifeng Hu,Yuncheng Jiang,Sijie Mai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于抑郁症自动检测的细粒度多模态框架，引入二元优势加权排序损失，通过优势加权分离和优势加权紧凑性两大机制优化潜在空间分布，在 D-vlog 和 LMVD 数据集上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对音视频多模态抑郁症检测中特征分布重叠、决策边界不稳健等难点，旨在通过深度跨模态融合与难样本挖掘重构潜在有序结构，提升检测精度。

Method: 设计包含时序编码器与互惠变换器的细粒度多模态框架实现深度跨模态融合；核心创新为二元优势加权排序损失：优势加权分离基于成对预测差异矩阵动态挖掘并加权困难样本对，优势加权紧凑性最小化类内方差使特征聚类向类中心收敛。

Result: 在 D-vlog 和 LMVD 两个公开数据集上进行大量实验，模型通过优先处理困难样本对重构潜在有序结构，实现了最先进的抑郁症检测性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了多模态特征分布重叠与决策边界脆弱的问题，二元优势加权排序损失的双重机制显著提升了潜在空间的判别能力，为音视频抑郁症自动检测提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Automatic depression detection using audio-visual data faces significant challenges, particularly in disentangling overlapping feature distributions and establishing robust decision boundaries. To address this, we propose a fine-grained multimodal framework featuring a temporal encoder and a mutual transformer to facilitate deep cross-modal fusion. Our core contribution is the Binary Advantage-weighting Ranking Loss, which optimizes the latent space distribution through two complementary mechanisms: Advantage-weighted Separation, which mines hard pairs by computing a pairwise prediction difference matrix and dynamically weighting them based on their difficulty; and Advantage-weighted Compactness, which minimizes intra-class variance to force features to cluster around their respective class centers. Extensive experiments on D-vlog and LMVD demonstrate that our model reconstructs the latent ordinal structure by prioritizing hard pairs, thereby achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [63] [PCBWorld: A Benchmark Environment for Engine-Grounded PCB Design Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05915)
*Hyungseok Song,Junseok Park,Won-Seok Choi,Seohui Bae,Han-Seul Jeong,Youngjoon Park,Soonyoung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PCBWorld 是一个基于 KiCad EDA 引擎的开源 PCB 布线环境，支持 RL 策略和工具使用的 LLM 代理通过引擎原生操作交互式布线，并提供合成与真实数据集及引擎验证的评估指标。实验显示该环境中的代理显著优于基线方法，且仅在合成板训练的 RL 策略能零样本迁移到真实板，接近基于规则的路由器性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的 PCB 布线方法仍落后于基于规则的路由器，缺乏一个能像人类工程师一样利用 EDA 引擎原生操作和 DRC 反馈进行交互式布线的标准化环境与基准。

Method: 构建 PCBWorld 环境，基于 KiCad 引擎提供原生布线操作与 DRC 反馈；配套 PCBWorld-Bench 提供三类数据集（两类可控合成实例和 679 块真实开源板），并定义八个引擎验证的评估指标；支持 RL 策略和工具使用型 LLM 代理在环境中交互式布线。

Result: 在 PCBWorld 中训练的代理持续优于网格动作 RL 策略和开环 LLM 基线；仅在合成板训练的 RL 策略能零样本迁移至真实板，性能接近基于规则的路由器。

Conclusion: PCBWorld 的引擎接地、交互式范式为提升 RL 和 LLM 代理的 PCB 布线能力提供了有前景的基础设施。

Abstract: PCB routing is the task of connecting the nets of a board with copper traces under strict design rules, yet learning-based methods still lag behind rule-based routers. We introduce PCBWorld, an open-source engine-grounded PCB routing environment built on the KiCad EDA engine. As a human engineer does, agents in PCBWorld interactively route a board through the engine's native operations, using its Design Rule Check (DRC) feedback to keep the routing within the design rules. The environment supports both RL policies and tool-using LLM agents. Alongside the environment, PCBWorld-Bench provides three dataset families in KiCad's native board format (.kicad_pcb), covering two types of controllable synthetic instances and 679 real open-source boards. It scores any completed board with eight engine-checked evaluation metrics, regardless of the routing method. In our experiments, agents in PCBWorld consistently outperformed grid-action RL policies and open-loop LLM baselines, and an RL policy trained only on synthetic boards transferred zero-shot to real boards, approaching rule-based routers. These results position the engine-grounded, interactive approach of PCBWorld as a promising foundation for advancing the routing ability of both RL and LLM agents.

</details>


### [64] [SearchEyes: Towards Frontier Multimodal Deep Search Intelligence via Search World Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05943)
*Zhengbo Jiao,Yiming Cheng,Yilei Jiang,Kaituo Feng,Rui Huang,Tianyi Jiang,Juanxi Tian,Jiapeng li,Qunzhong Wang,Tailai Chen,Qianshan Wei,Chuan Xiao,Shanyu Rong,Yangfu Li,Yanhan Zhou,Yunpu Ma,Yifan Zhang,Xiangyu Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchEyes提出了一种基于类型知识图谱的统一模拟搜索世界，通过感知-知识链(PKC)采样多跳路径并保留跳级实体元数据，结合跳锚定策略优化(HaPO)实现步级奖励分配，解决了多模态搜索代理训练中数据、环境、奖励脱节的问题，在六个多模态知识密集型基准上达到开源SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态搜索代理训练管道将训练数据构建、搜索环境和奖励信号独立设计，导致合成的结构化元数据被丢弃、环境依赖不可复现的外部引擎、且强化学习奖励在轨迹层面稀疏，难以支持多跳推理的有效训练。

Method: 1. 以类型知识图谱为骨干构建自包含的模拟搜索世界，统一数据、环境、奖励三组件。2. 提出感知-知识链(PKC)在Wikidata5M的视觉-知识交集上采样受约束的多跳路径，保留跳级实体元数据，同时定义搜索世界和步级奖励锚点。3. 提出跳锚定策略优化(HaPO)，直接复用这些锚点进行步级归因，无需单独训练过程奖励模型。

Result: 在六个多模态知识密集型基准实验中，SearchEyes在开源多模态搜索代理中达到SOTA性能，SearchEyes-27B比最强开源基线平均提升6.2分。

Conclusion: SearchEyes通过知识图谱统一的模拟搜索世界、PKC采样保留的结构化元数据以及HaPO的步级奖励机制，有效解决了多模态搜索代理训练中的结构断层问题，显著提升了多跳推理能力和训练效率。

Abstract: Training multimodal search agents to perform multi-hop reasoning remains challenging due to a fundamental structural disconnect: existing pipelines construct training data, search environments, and reward signals independently, causing synthesized structural metadata to be discarded, environments to rely on irreproducible external engines, and RL rewards to remain sparse at the trajectory level. We present \textbf{SearchEyes}, which uses a typed knowledge graph as the backbone of a \emph{simulated search world} that unifies all three components. We propose \textbf{Perception-Knowledge Chains (PKC)} to sample constrained multi-hop paths over the visual-knowledge intersection of Wikidata5M, retaining hop-level entity metadata that simultaneously defines a self-contained search world and step-level reward anchors. We further propose \textbf{Hop-Anchored Policy Optimization (HaPO)}, which reuses these anchors for step-level credit assignment without a separately trained process reward model. Experiments on six multimodal knowledge-intensive benchmarks show that SearchEyes achieves state-of-the-art performance among open-source multimodal search agents, with SearchEyes-27B improving over the strongest open-source baseline by 6.2 points on average.%

</details>


### [65] [Integrating knowledge graphs and multilingual scholarly corpora for domain-adaptive LLMs in SSH](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05956)
*Adam Faci,Alessio Miaschi,Anne Combe,Pascal Cuxac,Francesca Frontini,Nicolas Larrousse,Stéphane Pouyllau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了一个在欧洲项目LLMs4EU和ALT-EDIC基础设施中开发的用例，旨在将基础模型适配至社会科学与人文（SSH）研究实践，支持问答、比较文档分析和文献综述等任务，并建立了包含定量基准测试和定性专家评估的评估框架，探索领域敏感且合规的生成式AI如何在保持可靠性和认识论责任的前提下支持SSH学术研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型（LLMs）集成到科学研究工作流中，特别是用于书目发现和文献综合，给社会科学与人文（SSH）带来了方法论、认识论和监管层面的挑战，如学科多样性、多语言源获取和结果评估等问题，因此需要开发适配SSH研究实践的领域敏感、合规感知的生成式AI。

Method: 在LLMs4EU项目和ALT-EDIC基础设施中开发用例，将基础模型适配至SSH研究实践，支持问答、比较文档分析和文献综述等任务。评估框架遵循LLMs4EU协议，包括独立的定量基准测试（检索、摘要、可追溯性和幻觉检测）以及由数字人文专家小组进行的定性评估。同时将模型适配嵌入研究基础设施和结构化的法律伦理合规框架中。

Result: 该用例展示了如何通过领域敏感和合规感知的生成式AI支持SSH奖学，同时保持可靠性和认识论责任。定量基准测试和定性专家评估相结合，验证了模型在检索、摘要、可追溯性和幻觉检测等方面的性能，并获得了数字人文专家的认可。

Conclusion: 将模型适配嵌入研究基础设施和法律伦理合规框架，可以开发出适合SSH学科特点的生成式AI工具，在保证可靠性和认识论责任的前提下，有效支持书目发现、文献综合等研究任务，为SSH领域的AI应用提供了方法论范式。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into scientific research workflows, particularly for bibliographic discovery and literature synthesis, raises significant methodological, epistemic and regulatory challenges for the Social Sciences and Humanities (SSH), especially with regard to disciplinary diversity, multilingual access to sources and the evaluation of results. This paper presents an on-going use case developed within the European project LLMs4EU and the ALT-EDIC infrastructure, aimed at adapting foundation models to SSH research practices and supporting tasks such as question answering, comparative document analysis and literature review. The evaluation framework follows the LLMs4EU protocol and encompasses both independent quantitative benchmarking (retrieval, summarisation, traceability and hallucination detection) and a qualitative assessment involving a panel of Digital Humanities experts. By embedding model adaptation within research infrastructures and a structured legal and ethical compliance framework, the use case explores how domain-sensitive and regulation-aware generative AI can support SSH scholarship while preserving reliability and epistemic responsibility.

</details>


### [66] [Auto-DSM Under the Lens: A Black-Box Evaluation Framework for LLM-Based DSM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05985)
*Niels Potters,Theo Hofman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出黑盒评估框架，系统评估大语言模型从结构化技术文档生成设计结构矩阵（DSM）的能力，基于单次与多次运行的结构、分类、稳定性指标及综合质量分，实验表明LLM在结构良好输入下可生成合理DSM并具高复现性，但对歧义、依赖定义不一致、提示词敏感，揭示幻觉与弃权失败的系统性来源，为Auto-DSM管道审计与MBSE集成提供基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Auto-DSM流程多为闭源，缺乏透明、可复现的基准评估方法，阻碍了LLM驱动的DSM自动化在模型化系统工程（MBSE）中的可靠集成。

Method: 构建黑盒评估框架，将生成的DSM（GEN-DSM）与人工验证的真值DSM（GT-DSM）对比；采用单次与多次运行视角，结合结构指标（完整性、正确性、耦合密度）、分类指标（选择性准确率、弃权覆盖率）及稳定性指标（熵、Fleiss' κ），并提出综合质量分Q；在虚构抽象系统与真实冰箱分解两个数据集上进行控制实验，涵盖措辞变化、参数-数据集对齐及系统复杂度。

Result: 实验显示LLM在结构良好输入下能生成结构合理的DSM且复现性高，但对歧义、依赖定义不一致及提示词表述敏感；系统性识别出幻觉与弃权失败的来源。

Conclusion: 该框架为Auto-DSM管道提供透明审计基准，奠定LLM分解方法融入MBSE工作流的基础，同时揭示当前LLM驱动DSM自动化的潜力与局限。

Abstract: This paper presents a black-box evaluation framework to systematically assess the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate Design Structure Matrices (DSMs) from structured technical documentation. Motivated by the closed-source nature of current Auto-DSM pipelines, the framework introduces a reproducible methodology that benchmarks generated DSMs (GEN-DSMs) against manually validated ground-truth matrices (GT-DSMs). The evaluation integrates both single-run and multi-run perspectives, combining structural metrics (Completeness, Correctness, Coupling Density), classification metrics (Selective Accuracy, Abstention Coverage), and stability measures (Entropy, Fleiss' $κ$). To synthesize these aspects, a Composite Quality Score (Q) is proposed. Controlled experiments are conducted on two datasets: a fictive abstract system and a real-world refrigerator decomposition, covering variations in phrasing, parameter-dataset alignment, and system complexity. Results show that LLMs can produce structurally plausible DSMs and achieve high reproducibility under well-structured inputs, but remain sensitive to ambiguity, inconsistent dependency definitions, and prompt formulation. The findings highlight systematic sources of hallucination and abstention failure, demonstrating both the potential and current limitations of LLM-driven DSM automation. The proposed framework provides a transparent benchmark for auditing Auto-DSM pipelines and establishes foundations for integrating LLM-based decomposition methods into model-based systems engineering (MBSE) workflows.

</details>


### [67] [AgoraSim: A Hybrid Agent-Based Modeling Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05999)
*Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgoraSim 是一个混合智能体建模框架，将 LLM 智能体与经典 ABM 相结合，解决 LLM 模拟难以与显式社会动力学对比、易被过度解读为预测的问题。它支持多模态输入转可编辑 ABM 配置、比例可控的混合智能体群体（LLM、VLM、自定义端点、随机、经典智能体）、统一结构化决策对象、以及与经典参考动力学的对比，提供本地 UI、Python SDK/CLI 和 REST API，帮助用户检查场景轨迹、对比建模假设、识别需实证验证的案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体模拟虽易于实例化自然语言社会场景，但其输出常被过度解读为预测，且难以与显式的社会动力学模型进行系统对比，缺乏可审计、可对比的结构化框架。

Method: 构建混合 ABM 框架 AgoraSim：1) 将文本/多模态制品解析为可编辑的 ABM 配置；2) 运行比例可控的混合智能体群体（LLM、视觉语言模型、自定义端点、随机、经典智能体）；3) 所有智能体发射共享结构化决策对象，实现通用动作空间、交互协议、指标与审计记录；4) 同场景下与匹配的经典参考动力学对比；5) 暴露本地 UI、Python SDK/CLI、REST API 供检查与对比。

Result: AgoraSim 实现了 LLM 智能体与经典 ABM 的统一建模、对比与审计，支持场景轨迹检查、建模假设对比，并能识别出需要实证验证的关键案例。

Conclusion: AgoraSim 通过混合智能体建模与结构化决策对象，弥合了 LLM 模拟与经典社会动力学分析的鸿沟，为场景导向的社会反应分析提供了可控、可对比、可审计的工程化框架。

Abstract: LLM-agent simulations make natural-language social scenarios easy to instantiate, but their outputs can be overread as predictions and are often difficult to compare with explicit social dynamics. We present AgoraSim, a hybrid agent-based modeling framework for scenario-oriented social reaction analysis. AgoraSim resolves textual or multimodal artifacts into editable ABM configurations, runs ratio-controlled populations that mix LLM, vision-language, custom-endpoint, random, and classical agents, and compares the same scenario against matched classical reference dynamics. All agents emit a shared structured decision object, enabling common action spaces, interaction protocols, metrics, and audit records. Exposed through a local UI, Python SDK/CLI, and REST API, AgoraSim helps users inspect scenario trajectories, compare modeling assumptions, and identify cases that warrant empirical validation.

</details>


### [68] [Information Limits and Attractor Dynamics in Economies of Frontier LLM Agents: A Pre-Registered Test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06001)
*Cheng Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过预注册实验测试了两个关于多智能体系统的定量预测：基于信息论的财富增长容量区和均场残差缩放定律。结果1确认了差距定律等信息论预测；结果2显示均场模型假设的噪声维持分散机制在LLM群体中不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证耦合多智能体系统中两个理论预测——基于信息论的市场耦合财富增长容量区，以及激励和控制杠杆下群体错位的均场残差缩放定律——在前沿语言模型智能体（Claude Opus 4.8）组成的小型经济体中的实证表现。

Method: 采用预注册的两部分实验，所有预测、接受带和决策规则在运行前冻结于公开Git链中；使用缓存的模型输出机械重新推导所有报告数字；实验成本$138.76，可从缓存零成本复现。第一部分测试 pari-mutuel 耦合经济中的信息论容量区；第二部分测试72次群体运行中的均场残差缩放定律。

Result: 结果1确认：差距定律 G_a - G_b = I_a - I_b 在四种感知结构中最坏情况误差46毫奈特（预注册带50）；联盟价值在条件独立信道处为次模块化，XOR协同控制使其超模块化增加0.62≥ln2/2奈特；联合增长上界 G_S ≤ H(X) 精确成立；最知情智能体在5个市场种子中的4个吸收几乎全部财富池。结果2结构性否定：残差缩放测试返回'域未找到'，所有72次运行中目标分散度坍缩（V→0，最大4.85低于冻结底线5.31），群体对两个杠杆的响应为跨越支配边界的阶跃函数而非平滑响应，边界附近细胞双稳态且依赖种子选择；没有任何能力水平的LLM群体实现平滑均场模型假设的噪声维持分散机制。

Conclusion: 实验确认了信息论财富增长预测，但反驳了均场残差缩放定律在LLM群体中的适用性，揭示了当前语言模型智能体群体缺乏噪声维持的目标分散机制，具有双稳态和阶跃响应特征。完整协议、预注册链、调用缓存和分析代码已公开。

Abstract: We report a pre-registered, two-part experiment on small economies of frontier language-model agents (Claude Opus 4.8), testing two quantitative predictions about coupled multi-agent systems: an information-theoretic capacity region for wealth growth under market coupling, and a mean-field residual-scaling law for population misalignment under incentive and control levers. All predictions, acceptance bands, and decision rules were frozen in a public git chain before any run; every reported number re-derives mechanically from cached model outputs; the entire experiment cost $138.76 in metered API spend and is re-runnable at zero cost from the cache.
